(2) Degree-one census (1,2,1,1) at the level of characters (chi_E = chi_1 + 2 chi_W + chi_V2 + chi_Wsgn) and eq:degree-one-commutant as an explicit unital algebra isomorphism C x M_2(C) x C x C ~= degreeOneCommutant (dimension 7). (4) Neither algebra contains a unital copy of M_3(C) (degree_one_colour_no_go). Renderings disclosed: 'every finite word depth' is rendered by quantifying over all unitary matrix representations of finite groups on finite carriers (the word-depth carriers rho_r(S_4) are instances); the sector set iota indexes the irreducibles that actually occur (zero-multiplicity irreducibles contribute no block, as in the paper's zero summands); the multiplicity vectors (1,2,1,1) and (2,2,1,1) are established as character identities plus the explicit block algebras rather than by matching the sectors of the general clause with the labels lambda in hat S_4 (that matching would need character orthogonality; it is not needed for the two displayed censuses, which are proved concretely). \|\| Re-verified against the NEW statement (renamed A_tet, complete five-sector census in the order eq:complete-S4-census, and the merged flat-termination clause). (a) Five-entry censuses: the Lean degree-one census is the character identity χ_E = χ_1 + 2χ_W + χ_{V2} + χ_{W⊗sgn} (degree_one_multiplicity_census), i.e. (1,0,2,1,1) with the sign entry 0 because the sign character is absent; likewise (2,0,2,1,1) for the private line (private_line_multiplicity_census). (b) The clause 'exact exhaustion of the relative commutant for the proposed sector assignment requires precisely m_r = (3,0,2,1,1), a nonzero sign entry adds a summand' is read off eq:external-isotypic-commutant as rendered (Wᴴ A'_tet W = multBlockSet I J = ⊕_b 1 ⊗ M_{J b}(C) over the sectors present, so the commutant is M_3 ⊕ M_2 ⊕ C ⊕ C on the assigned sectors iff \|J\| = (3,2,1,1) there and no sign sector is present); it is not displayed as a separate Lean statement. (c) Flat termination (formerly thm:flat-depth-colour, lem:flat-word-span, cor:flat-multiplicity-obstruction): rank K_r = rank K_{r+1} for the labelled source-word Gram K_r = W_rᴴ W_r ⟹ V_s = V_r for all s ≥ r and every letter preserves V_r (flat_word_span_of_gram_rank, sourceWordGram literal form in flat_depth_colour_obstruction_of_word_gram), every isotypic multiplicity frozen (flat_word_span_multiplicity_constant), r_col^birth = colourBirthDepth in ℕ∞ with m_{0,r} = m0, a flat depth with m_{0,r} ≤ 2 gives no external-trivial M_3 at any depth and r_col^birth = ∞ (flat_depth_colour_obstruction_of_word_gram), and the general terminal obstruction 'm_{λ,r} < n at a flat depth ⟹ no unital M_n on that multiplicity space at any depth' (flat_multiplicity_obstruction_of_gram_rank, no_unital_Mn_of_multiplicity_lt). Scoped: arbitrary family of external symmetry operators ρ on a finite-dimensional carrier (S_4 is a special case). |
| `thm:colour-bridge` | proposition | Colour bridge, weak router, and central separation | [`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeed.internal_seed_dichotomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalCommutantSeedExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeed.adjoin_ne_top_of_omega_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalCommutantSeedExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeed.omegaCol`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalCommutantSeedExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.WeakReset.two_lines_generate`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakResetGenerationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.central_separation`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.central_separation_dichotomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.central_separation_criterion`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.etaCen_pos_of_central_projector`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.finrank_blockAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.finrank_lockedAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.lockedAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.etaCen`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean) | Merged statement (former thm:colour-bridge + lem:weak-M2 + thm:central-separation), all three proved. Colour bridge: on C^3 = T + H_priv with generators p_T, the four private matrix units (the hypothesis that private words generate M_2(H_priv)), u and u^*: the generated algebra is M_3(C) iff omega_col(u) = \|\|p_H u p_T\|\|^2 + \|\|p_T u p_H\|\|^2 > 0 (InternalSeed.internal_seed_dichotomy, omegaCol); in the zero-bridge case the generators are block diagonal and the algebra != M_3 (adjoin_ne_top_of_omega_zero); equality with C + M_2 follows since the units of C + M_2 are among the generators but is not displayed as a separate statement. Weak router eq:weak-M2: WeakReset.two_lines_generate proves the forward direction (independent, nonorthogonal t, h, i.e. 0 < \|<t,h>\| < 1 for unit vectors, generate all of M_2(C)); scoped: the converse direction (generation forces 0 < \|<t,h>\| < 1) and the remark that a single rank-one projection generates only C^2 are not formalized (both are immediate: orthogonal or equal lines give a commutative algebra). Central separation: CentralSeparation.central_separation / central_separation_dichotomy (a unital star-subalgebra of M_3 + M_2 + C + C = blockAlgebra of M_7(C) surjecting onto the colour and weak factors is exactly blockAlgebra (finrank 15) or lockedAlgebra {(A,B,z,z)} (finrank 14), eq:central-locked-14), central_separation_criterion (for a generating bank b_j, B = blockAlgebra iff eta_cen = sum_j \|chi_1(b_j) - chi_2(b_j)\|^2 > 0 with chi_1, chi_2 the two scalar characters), etaCen_pos_of_central_projector (a represented scalar central support in the bank forces eta_cen > 0: independent scalar central supports suffice). Scoped: the subdirect-product step is proved directly on the concrete census carrier C^7 rather than invoking thm:subdirect-classification. |

SaturationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeedSaturation.omega7_bankWord7_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalSeedSaturationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeedSaturation.ReciprocalBridge`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalSeedSaturationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalSeedSaturation.reciprocalBridge_of_mass_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalSeedSaturationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalDeficit.internal_seed_saturation_concrete`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalDeficit.adjoin_both_eq_blockAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalDeficit.finrank_centre`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalDeficit.saturation_iff_finrank`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.central_separation_criterion`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_pos_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ActiveIsotypic.active_isotypic`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ActiveIsotypicExact.lean) | Proved (transport of the concrete generation theorem along an explicit isomorphism with the external relative commutant). internal_seed_saturation: for an external action rho : G ->* M_n(C) with isotypic decomposition data (iota, I, J, W) exactly as furnished by the parent thm:active-isotypic (W unitary, nonempty external factors, W^H A'_ext W = multBlockSet I J = (+)_b 1 (x) M_{J b}(C)), the census eq:SM-multiplicity-census rendered as e : iota ~= Fin 4 with \|J (e.symm k)\| = ![3,2,1,1] k, (I1) as 'the colour-supported represented word u has omega_col(u) > 0' OR 'u is the compression (bankWord7) of a nonzero returned word r_{g,k} = B^*(v_g (x) h^k)A of a reciprocal mediator bank' (ReciprocalBridge u; reciprocalBridge_of_mass_pos derives such a word from m_ret > 0 via mass_pos_iff of thm:reciprocal-return, and omega7_bankWord7_pos shows its H_priv <- T corner gives omega_col > 0), (I2) a weak router h, (I3)/(I4) the represented scalar sectors and central supports (part of the base generators): there is a unital C-algebra isomorphism Phi : blockAlgebra (= M_3 + M_2 + C + C on C^7) ~=_alg A'_ext := centralizer of C^*(rho(G)) such that the algebra generated by the transported represented words (Phi of baseGens u bridgeGens u u routerGen h) is A'_ext (eq:active-internal-algebra), dim_C A'_ext = 15, dim_C Z(A'_ext) = 4 (Subalgebra.center), and the two nonabelian sectors have 9 and 4 matrix entries (finrank of M_{J(e.symm 0)}, M_{J(e.symm 1)}). internal_seed_saturation_of_unitary packages this for every unitary rho with the decomposition data produced by external_isotypic. Infrastructure: multBlockHom (Pi_b M_{J b} -> M_{Sigma I x J}, injective, range multBlockSet), extEmbed (conjugation by W; range = A'_ext), censusEquiv : Fin 7 ~= Sigma_b J b matching blockOf to the sectors, blockHom7 with range blockAlgebra, censusCommutantEquiv. Renderings disclosed: (a) the decomposition data of the parent are hypotheses (discharged by external_isotypic in _of_unitary); (b) the represented words are modelled by the explicit generator set of the concrete theorem transported through Phi (private units, type line, scalar and central supports, bridge word, router), not as depth-r compressions; (c) the coherent realisation T + H_priv of the multiplicity-three sector is the coordinate choice made by censusEquiv, and which sector carries which S_4-irreducible is not tracked (only multiplicities); (d) 'distinguished by matrix sizes' is rendered as the entry counts 9 and 4 of the two nonabelian sectors. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. \|\| Re-verified against the NEW statement: the census is now written as the complete five-sector vector m_r = (3,0,2,1,1) in the order eq:complete-S4-census (trivial, sgn, W, W⊗sgn, V_2). The Lean census hypothesis (e : ι ≃ Fin 4 with \|J (e.symm k)\| = ![3,2,1,1] k) indexes the sectors PRESENT in the isotypic decomposition (all J b nonempty), so the zero sign-isotypic entry is rendered as the absence of a sign sector; the four listed sizes are the same. Cross-references were renamed (Proposition thm:reciprocal-return for m_ret > 0, Proposition thm:colour-bridge for η_cen > 0: the Lean routes are ReciprocalBridge / reciprocalBridge_of_mass_pos and central_separation_criterion, unchanged). |

Represented words: baseGens = {E_00, private units E_11,E_12,E_21,E_22 (colour three-space initially M_2+C), one weak rank-one projection E_33 (coordinates with t = e_3), the weak central support E_33+E_44, and the scalar sectors E_55, E_66 (colour and scalar central supports are in the span)}; optional colour-supported bridge word u with u^H (bridge iff omega7 u > 0; no bridge = omega7 u = 0, which includes u = 0); optional weak router \|h><h\| with h supported on {3,4}, h_3 != 0 != h_4 (0 < \|<t,h>\| < 1). internal_deficit_alternatives: finrank of the generated unital algebra is 9 / 11 / 13 / 15 in the four rows; adjoin_both_eq_blockAlgebra / saturation_iff_finrank: the terminal value 15 is equivalent to saturation A = M_3+M_2+C+C (blockAlgebra). Method: each row's algebra equals the pattern algebra of an explicit block partition (dimension = number of same-block pairs, finrank_patternAlgebra), with colour_units_of_bridge (thm:colour-bridge extraction) and weak_units_of_router (lem:weak-M2 with one diagonal projection) supplying the matrix units. The 14-dimensional scalar-locked branch is CentralSeparationExact.lockedAlgebra / central_separation (thm:central-separation). Scoped: weak projection t diagonalised (WLOG up to the unitary of the weak plane); words given as a set of generators of an Algebra.adjoin (star-closure supplied by including u^H and the fact that all other generators are self-adjoint). Re-verified 30 Sep 2026: the new version writes the census as the complete S_4 x reversal census m_r = (3,0,2,1,1) (the extra 0 is the absent type) and cites thm:colour-bridge instead of the former thm:central-separation for the 14-dimensional scalar-locked branch; the mathematical content and the Lean rendering are unchanged. |

[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.gauge_group_phase_sensitive`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.determinant_phase_rigidity`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.packetAction_toMatrix_sub_hsNormSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.hDet_pos_iff_packetStabilizer_eq_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.extendedStabilizer`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.mem_extendedStabilizer_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.extendedStabilizer_eq_ker_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.MarkedSectorPacket.toMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.hsNormSq`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/DeterminantPhaseRigidityExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.determinantSeedStabilizer_eq_SMGaugeGroup`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/OperationalDeterminantSource.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.operational_determinant_source`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/OperationalDeterminantSource.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.packetStabilizer_eq_ker_of_hDet_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/OperationalDeterminantSource.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.packetStabilizer_eq_top_of_hDet_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/OperationalDeterminantSource.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OperationalDeterminantSource.SMGaugeGroup_ne_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/OperationalDeterminantSource.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.smGaugeQuotientEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGaugeQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.sm_group`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGroup.lean) | Proved in full for chi(A,B) = det A det B on U(3) x U(2) (gauge_group_phase_sensitive), with each clause also proved for an arbitrary group with a unitary character chi. (1) eq:SM-gauge-group: the stabilizer of a nonzero phase-sensitive seed tau is S(U(3)xU(2)) = ker chi = SMGaugeGroup (determinantSeedStabilizer_eq_SMGaugeGroup) and SMGaugeGroup is isomorphic to (SU(3)xSU(2)xU(1))/Z_6 (smGaugeQuotientEquiv, sm_group). (2) eq:operational-determinant-group in BOTH directions: h_det > 0 <-> Stab(J) = ker chi (hDet_pos_iff_packetStabilizer_eq_ker, the reverse direction using ker chi != U(3)xU(2), SMGaugeGroup_ne_top); if h_det = 0 the whole group fixes the marked packet, whatever the diagonal block J_DD (packetStabilizer_eq_top_of_hDet_zero, operational_determinant_source). (3) eq:determinant-phase-rigidity: for the full Hermitian packet J = (J_00, J_0D; J_0D^*, J_DD) (MarkedSectorPacket.toMatrix) acted on by 1 on V_0 and chi^{-1} on S_nat (packetAction), \|\|R(g) J R(g)^* - J\|\|^2_HS = 2 \|chi(g)^{-1} - 1\|^2 h_det (packetAction_toMatrix_sub_hsNormSq) = 8 h_det sin^2(phi/2) when chi(g) = e^{i phi} (determinant_phase_rigidity). (4) Packet extension: for a larger marked packet carrying further retained components x_i with any G-action, the stabilizer is the intersection Stab(J) meet inf_i Stab(x_i) (extendedStabilizer, mem_extendedStabilizer_iff), and when h_det > 0 it equals ker chi iff every x_i is ker chi-invariant (extendedStabilizer_eq_ker_iff). Scoping disclosed: the HS norm on the packet is the entrywise sum of \|.\|^2 (hsNormSq), matching hDet; the 'positive packet' hypothesis is not needed for any clause and is omitted. |

| `prop:faithful-SM-quotient` | proposition | Faithful matter realization of the determinant quotient | [`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.faithful_quotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.commonKernel_eq_zpowers`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.commonKernel_eq_smGaugeHom_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.orderOf_z6`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.z6_entries`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.commonKernel`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.repQ`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.mem_smGaugeHom_ker_iff_centralZ6`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGaugeQuotientExact.lean) | Proved. The seven multiplets Q:(3,2)_1, u^c:(3bar,1)_-4, d^c:(3bar,1)_2, L:(1,2)_-3, e^c:(1,1)_6, H:(1,2)_3, nu^c:(1,1)_0 are realized as monoid homomorphisms from SMGaugeCover = (SU(3) x SU(2)) x Circle into matrix monoids (Kronecker product for Q, entrywise conjugation for 3bar, z^y phase with y = 6Y). commonKernel (intersection of the seven kernels) = smGaugeHom.ker = Subgroup.zpowers z6 with z6 = (e^{2 pi i/3} I_3, -I_2, e^{i pi/3}) (z6_entries gives the displayed entries), orderOf z6 = 6 (K = Z_6). faithful_quotient: the joint packet representation has kernel K = <z6> = ker(G~ -> S(U(3)xU(2))), and the descended representation of G~/K (QuotientGroup.kerLift of the units-valued hom) is injective, i.e. the packet is faithful precisely for G~/K, the group of thm:gauge-group (smGaugeQuotientEquiv). Proof follows the paper: e^c gives z^6 = 1, L gives g_2 = z^3 I = z^-3 I, d^c gives g_3 = z^2 I; then all seven act trivially on the central Z_6. |
| `cor:anomaly-faithful-group` | corollary | Faithful global group from anomaly freedom | [`RenewalGeometry.anomaly_forced_weights`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/AnomalyForcedWeights.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.faithful_quotient`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.commonKernel_eq_smGaugeHom_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.commonKernel_eq_zpowers`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FaithfulSMQuotient.jointRep`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FaithfulSMQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.smGaugeQuotientEquiv`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGaugeQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.smGaugeHom_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGaugeQuotientExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.mem_smGaugeHom_ker_iff_centralZ6`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SMGaugeQuotientExact.lean) | Proved as an assembly of existing theorems (the corollary's proof is exactly this composition). anomaly_forced_weights: simultaneous vanishing of the four local anomalies iff 3a + 2b = 0, integral solutions (a,b) = t(-2,3), primitive coprime solutions exactly +-(-2,3) ('up to simultaneous sign'). FaithfulSMQuotient.faithful_quotient: the represented chiral tensor packet Q + u^c + d^c + L + e^c + H + nu^c (jointRep, charges from the table y = (1,4,-2,-3,-6,3,0)) has kernel exactly zpowers z6 = Z_6 = ker smGaugeHom and the descended representation of G~/K is injective (prop:faithful-SM-quotient). smGaugeQuotientEquiv: SMGaugeCover / smGaugeHom.ker ≃* SMGaugeGroup = S(U(3) x U(2)) with smGaugeHom surjective and the kernel the central Z_6 {(z^2 I_3, z^-3 I_2, z) : z^6 = 1}; hence G_phys := G~/K = (SU(3) x SU(2) x U(1))/Z_6 ≅ S(U(3) x U(2)) as a group-theoretic identity with no determinant-source hypothesis. For n_Q > 0: Delta_lin = 0 <-> all five multiplicities equal a common g > 0 (deltaLin_eq_zero_iff, eq:linear-anomaly-common-count; nonnegativity of the other multiplicities is not needed and is omitted); on that branch the cubic U(1)^3 anomaly of the packet (multiplicityCubicAnomaly: sum_f n_f dim(f) y_f^3 with the anomaly-forced weights (a,b) = (-2,3)) equals g times the one-generation cubic anomaly cubicCentralAnomaly (-2) 3 of thm:hypercharge and hence vanishes (multiplicityCubicAnomaly_common, _eq_zero_of_common); the weak-doublet count 3 n_Q + n_L equals 4g and is even (weakDoubletCount_eq); Delta_lin != 0 -> Delta_lin >= 1 (deltaLin_ge_one_of_ne_zero, eq:linear-anomaly-integer-gap); and on the Delta_lin = 0 branch one multiplicity certified as 3 forces the charged multiplicity vector (3,3,3,3,3), i.e. the charged carrier 3Q+3u+3d+3L+3e (charged_carrier_eq_three_of_certified). Rendering disclosed: the tail's 'no future-visible charged mirror or exotic sector' is the inventory hypothesis Delta_inv^F = 0 of thm:canonical-chiral-projectors (the future-visible nontrivial gauge carrier consists of the five target chiral types), under which the charged carrier is exactly the multiplicity vector; it is not re-encoded here. Gauge-trivial singlets are not part of the vector. |

injective row choices of each I; non-injective choices vanish); (3) eq:howe-enhancement-locus: {Delta = 0} = {theta : some X commuting with all c_j(theta) lies outside M}, i.e. C*(c(theta))' strictly contains M (kernel of D(theta) = additional commutant inside K, howeDiscriminant_eq_zero_iff); (4) if Delta(theta_0) != 0 at one point then the enhancement locus is Lebesgue-null with empty interior and for every open U the exact set {theta in U : Delta != 0} is open, dense in U (U subset of its closure) and of full measure in U (new infrastructure PolynomialZeroSetNull: the zero set of a nonzero real polynomial on R^p is null, by induction on p with Fubini (Measure.measure_prod_null) and finiteness of roots of one-variable polynomials; empty interior via IsOpenPosMeasure). howe_discriminant_analytic proves the real-analytic clause: for entries AnalyticOnNhd R on an open preconnected U containing an exact point, interior{Delta=0} meets U trivially and the exact set is open and dense in U (identity theorem; Delta analytic via analyticOnNhd_det). Scoping disclosed: C*(c)' strictly containing M is rendered through the algebraic complement K spanned by E (the paper's orthonormal basis of M^perp is the special case); the paper's 'analytic component' is rendered as an open preconnected region; degrees are total degrees of the complex coefficient polynomials. |

Galois clause eq:incidence-closure-galois: starClosed_le_iff_matCommutant_subset states, for two star-closed unital subalgebras O_1, O_2 of a finite matrix algebra (the closed coefficient-action algebras O_Y, O_Ytilde, which are C*-algebras by definition eq:coefficient-action-algebra) and M_type := commutant (eq:coefficient-quiver-duality M_type = O_Y'), O_1 <= O_2 <-> O_2' subset O_1' -- exactly the paper's proof (commutants reverse inclusions: matCommutant_anti; finite bicommutant closure reverses them back: double_commutant); starClosed_eq_iff_matCommutant_eq is the 'Thus' clause (equality of closed coefficient-action algebras <-> equality of exact multiplicity algebras). Typing refinement: typingRefinement_exact, for A_type^(1) subset A_type^(2), A_i = jointActionAlgebra A_Cl A_type^(i) Y = C*(A_Cl, A_type^(i), Y, Y^*) and M_i = matCommutant A_i, gives A_1 <= A_2, M_2 subset M_1 (eq:typing-refinement-monotone), M_1 = M_2 <-> A_1 = A_2, and A_1 = A_2 <-> A_type^(2) subset A_1 (using A_2 = C*(A_1, A_type^(2)) = jointActionAlgebra_refined_le). Scoped: the paper's hypothesis A_type^(i) subset A_Cl' is not needed and not assumed; carrier index n : Type (universe 0, inherited from matCommutant_anti); the Galois clause is stated for abstract star-closed O_Y rather than through a slice encoding of O_Y (the slice encoding is def:typed-multiplicity's record; M_type = O_Y' is thm:main-duality). |

Y_e : K_lambda (x) M_lambda -> K_mu (x) M_mu is a matrix on product index types; its prescribed carrier coefficient space is carrierCoefficientSpace Y = span of the carrier slices (i,j) -> Y (i,a) (j,b) = all (id (x) psi)(Y). Primary clause: multiplicityOne_incidenceFactorization_exact -- if this space lies in the line C D with D != 0 (weaker than 'is the line', so the theorem is stronger; carrierCoefficientSpace_eq_of_kronecker shows equality holds for Y != 0), there is a unique F with Y = D (x)_k F (eq:incidence-factorization), uniqueness relative to D by kronecker_left_cancel. 'In particular' clause: carrierCoefficientSpace_le_of_equivariance / multiplicityOne_incidenceFactorization_of_equivariance -- a declared equivariance (L_q (x) 1) Y = Y (R_q (x) 1) (trivial on the multiplicity factors) whose carrier intertwiners are multiples of D forces the carrier coefficient space into C D. Reduction clause: multiplicitySliceSpan_kronecker shows that on this branch the intrinsic coefficient space B_e (local encoding multiplicitySliceSpan = span of the multiplicity slices (a,b) -> Y (i,a) (j,b), i.e. of all (phi (x) id)(Y), def:incidence-coefficients) equals the line C F; typedMultiplicity_line_reduction: the equations of eq:typed-multiplicity over B_e = C F (R_mu B = B R_lambda, R_lambda B^* = B^* R_mu for all B in C F) are equivalent to R_mu F = F R_lambda and R_lambda F^* = F^* R_mu; typedMultiplicity_carrier_reduction gives the same reduction at carrier level (commutation of 1 (x) R with D (x) F and its adjoint). Scoped: finite index types; multiplicitySliceSpan is a local matrix-coordinate encoding of B_e, to be identified with the def:typed-multiplicity encoding by the coordinator (not modified here to avoid clashing with the concurrent batch). |

via a symmetrised Cauchy-Binet identity proved from scratch (factorial_mul_det_eq_sum: n! det(sum_r t_r conj(A_ri) A_rj) = sum over ALL row choices p : Fin n -> R of (prod_i t_{p i}) \|det A_p\|^2, non-injective p contributing 0, so no grouping by image is needed), applied to the stacked matrix of the constraint vectors v_{(e,k)} = D_e^* b_{e,k}; (3) the face criterion P_E(w) > 0 <-> f(S) = n (<-> M(S) = M(E) in the commutant setting, incidenceDet_pos_iff_enlargedCommutant_eq); (4) under f(E) = n, the least power of X_e in P_E is exactly kappa_e = n - f(E minus e): kappa_e <= alpha(e) for every monomial with nonzero coefficient (a row basis uses at most f(E minus e) rows from the other blocks) and some monomial attains it (extend a row basis of R(E minus e) by rows of block e, via exists_linearIndepOn_extension); (5) e essential (f(E minus e) < n) iff every monomial of P_E contains X_e. | Proved (record_nonidentifiability). Statement: on any 20-dimensional carrier m (Fintype.card m = 20), for any record factor with Hermitian orthogonal projectors Q_a summing to I and any 0 <= theta <= 1, there exist two six-edge families K^c, K^q (the completions I/sqrt18 + (i/3) H of the commuting family H^c on the twenty three-subsets and of the Clifford family H^q = sqrt(6/5) sum_mu O_{e mu} Gamma_mu (x) I_5 on C^4 (x) C^5, both reindexed to m) such that: both satisfy eq:six-edge-normalization (sum K^*K = I, sum K = sqrt2 I, linear independence); both resolved instruments L_0 = sqrt(1-theta) I, L_{e,a} = sqrt(theta) K_e (x) Q_a (kraus) are normalized; they have the same one-step effects, the same effect L_w^* L_w for every classical word w (the same resolved record channel at every word length; the explicit eq:record-word-effect formula (1-theta)^{n_0}(theta/6)^m I (x) Q_a / zero for disagreeing types is record_word_effect), the same one-letter Hilbert-Schmidt Gram Tr K_e^* K_f, and Choi matrices with the same characteristic polynomial (choiMat_charpoly_eq_of_hsGram_eq via Matrix.charpoly_mul_comm': the Choi matrix is B B^* for the matrix B of Choi vectors, so its spectrum with multiplicities is a function of the Gram B^*B = Tr L_j^* L_k, which the two instruments share); yet the unital algebras generated by the K_e are not isomorphic (not Nonempty of an AlgEquiv) and neither are their commutants (Subalgebra.centralizer). New concrete inputs: a Helmert frame (helmert, integer Helmert vectors over sqrt(k(k+1))) and a Clifford family on C^4 (gamma 0..3 and the chirality). Renderings disclosed: C*(K_e) is taken as Algebra.adjoin of the K_e (the generators are I/sqrt18 + (i/3)H_e with H_e Hermitian, so the *-algebra equals the algebra; finite dimension); nonisomorphism is proved by the commutative/noncommutative contrast (adjoin K^c = diagonal algebra C^20 from the previous file; adjoin K^q contains Gamma_0 (x) I_5 and Gamma_1 (x) I_5; commutant of K^c = diagonal matrices, commutant of K^q contains I (x) M_5(C)); the exact identification C*(K^q) = M_4(C) (x) I_5 of eq:inequivalent-system-algebras and the explicit eigenvalue list eq:matching-Choi-spectrum are not computed (not needed for the countertheorem: 'same nonzero Choi spectrum' is covered by equality of the characteristic polynomials). The record factor is an arbitrary finite type with orthogonal projectors (the paper's four rank-one projectors are a special case); theta is allowed in the closed interval [0,1]. |

Hypotheses: Asys = starAdjoin (range C) = starAdjoin (range A) (i.e. A_sys = C*(C_nu) = C*(A_1..A_s)), C(z_0) = alpha • 1, C(z_j) = beta_j • A_j, alpha ≠ 0, beta_j ≠ 0. Conclusion: pullbackAlgebra C = Asys and matCommutant (pullbackAlgebra C) = matCommutant Asys, both clauses of eq:pullback-reconstruction. Axioms: propext, Classical.choice, Quot.sound only. |
| `cor:finite-system-export` | corollary | Finite coherent system export | [`RenewalGeometry.finite_system_export`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteSystemExportExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.jointCommutatorCLM_opNorm_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteSystemExportExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.no_additional_commutant_direction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/FiniteSystemExportExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.ten_panel_reconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.reconstruction_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean) | Proved as the assembled theorem finite_system_export: for the normalized six-edge source class (sum K_e = sqrt2 I, sum Q_a = I, 0<theta<1, Helmert frame O^T O = I, O^T 1 = 0, O O^T = I - 11^T/6) (1) the ten quadratures reconstruct all six coefficients on the acted-on carrier E x R (D_mu (x) I = (2 sqrt3/sqrt(theta h))(P*(X_mu) + i P*(Y_mu)), K_e = I/sqrt18 + sum_mu O_{e mu} D_mu); (2) the linear reconstruction from measured panels with HS errors eps_X, eps_Y has HS coefficient error <= e_K = (2 sqrt3/sqrt(theta h)) (sum (eps_X+eps_Y)^2)^{1/2}; (3) the star-closed stacked commutator maps satisfy \|\|D_hat - D\|\|_{2->2} <= 2 sqrt2 e_K as an operator-norm inequality of continuous linear maps; (4) stopping test. Disclosed renderings of the paper's informal phrases: 'independently verified protected commutant' = a subspace M of the Hilbert-Schmidt space with M <= ker D (true map); its 'protected complement' = M-perp; 'measured least singular value s_hat' = the bound s_hat \|\|x\|\| <= \|\|D_hat x\|\| on M-perp; 'no additional commutant direction' = ker D = M exactly, equivalently every matrix commuting with all K_e (x) I and their adjoints lies in M. The true coefficient is compared as K_e (x) I_R on the common carrier. |
| `prop:five-kraus-freedom` | proposition | Five-Kraus freedom behind the six-edge normalization | [`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.six_edge_iff_five_kraus`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.coarse_channel_normalForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.starAdjoin_normalForm_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.five_kraus_freedom`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.HelmertFrame`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.SixEdgeNormalization`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.FiveKrausData`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.normalForm`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiveKraus.extracted`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiveKrausFreedomExact.lean) | Full statement on Matrix m m ℂ (finite-dimensional edge-system space) for a Helmert frame O given as a parameter with the three identities of eq:helmert-frame (structure HelmertFrame; the paper says 'choose O with ...', so the frame is a parameter, not constructed). six_edge_iff_five_kraus: eq:six-edge-normalization (∑K*K=I, ∑K=√2 I, K linearly independent) ⟺ ∃ B_1..B_5 with ∑B*B=I, I,B_1..B_5 linearly independent (Fin.cons 1 B) and K_e = I/√18 + √(2/3)∑O_{eμ}B_μ (normalForm). coarse_channel_normalForm: ∑K_e ρ K_e* = (1/3)ρ + (2/3)∑B_μ ρ B_μ*. starAdjoin_normalForm_eq: StarAlgebra.adjoin ℂ (range K) = StarAlgebra.adjoin ℂ (range B) (unital *-algebra generated = C*-algebra generated in finite dimension). five_kraus_freedom assembles all clauses with the explicit extraction B_μ = √(3/2)∑_e O_{eμ}K_e as in the paper's proof. |

gonal`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/RecordWitnessCommutingFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RecordWitness.clifford_record_data`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/RecordWitnessCliffordFamily.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RecordWitness.helmert`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/RecordWitnessConcrete.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.RecordWitness.cliffordSMST4`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/RecordWitnessConcrete.lean) | Proved (record_witness), for the two concrete lists K^c (on C^S, \|S\| = 20 three-subsets) and K^q (on C^4 x C^5, concrete Helmert frame `helmert`, concrete Clifford family `cliffordSMST4`) of eq:two-system-completions, any record projectors Q_a (a in a 4-element type) that are Hermitian, mutually orthogonal, sum to I and have trace 1 (the paper's nonzero orthogonal projections on H_R = C^4 summing to I), and any 0 <= theta <= 1: (i) eq:six-edge-normalization for both lists (completeness, coherent sum sqrt2 I, linear independence); (ii) eq:matching-record-data for both (K^*K = I/6, Tr K_e^* K_f = 10/3 / 2/3); (iii) eq:inequivalent-system-algebras: C*(K^c) = the diagonal subalgebra C^20 and C*(K^q) = M_4(C) tensor I_5 (adjoin_cliffordHq_eq_kronOne, via NCG.CommonOrigin.gamma_generates; C*(K) is rendered as the unital algebra generated by the K_e, which equals the *-algebra since K_e = I/sqrt18 + (i/3)H_e with H_e Hermitian); (iv) eq:record-word-effect in both realizations: a word with n_0 no-response and m accepted letters and one common accepted record type a has effect (1-theta)^{n_0}(theta/6)^m I_20 tensor Q_a, zero if the record types disagree (record_word_effect); (v) eq:matching-Choi-spectrum: both Choi matrices of the resolved instrument (25 Kraus operators L_0, L_{e,a} on H_E tensor H_R) have characteristic polynomial X^{(20 dim H_R)^2 - 24} (X - (20/3)(12-11 theta)) (X - 20 theta/3)^3 (X - 8 theta/3)^20, i.e. the same nonzero Choi spectrum {(20/3)(12-11theta)^(1), (20theta/3)^(3), (8theta/3)^(20)} (choi_charpoly_kraus, proved for any family with the shared record data via the factorization J = B M_theta B^* and Matrix.charpoly_mul_comm'). Scoping: theta in [0,1] instead of (0,1); the trace-one hypothesis on Q_a is what the paper's H_R = C^4 forces; the two realizations live on their own 20-dimensional carriers (the transported common-carrier version of the nonidentifiability statement is thm:record-nonidentifiability, RecordWitnessInstrument.lean:record_nonidentifiability). |
| `prop:ten-panel-reconstruction` | proposition | Explicit ten-panel reconstruction | [`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.ten_panel_reconstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.ten_panel_determines_coefficients`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.complementaryPullback_vecMulVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.cfc_sqrt_coefficientGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.pulledCoefficient_uniformEnv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.pulledCoefficient_contrastEnv`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean) | Proved in the paper's concrete frame: accepted operators A_{e,a} = K_e ⊗ Q_a (TenPanel.acceptedOp), Gram M_ϑ = ϑI + (1−ϑ)cc* (coefficientGram) with the explicit positive square root √ϑ(I−P) + √h P identified with Mathlib's CFC.sqrt (cfc_sqrt_coefficientGram), frame coefficients C_ν = Σ_j (M_ϑ^{1/2})_{jν} A_j, Stinespring map V = Σ C_ν ⊗ \|ν⟩, Heisenberg pullback 𝒫*(F) = V*(I⊗F)V, and eq:complementary-pullback 𝒫*(\|z⟩⟨w\|) = C(z)*C(w) proved for any finite coefficient family (complementaryPullback_vecMulVec). ten_panel_reconstruction gives both boxed formulas D_μ ⊗ I = (2√3/√(ϑh))(𝖷_μ + i𝖸_μ) and K_e = I/√18 + Σ_μ O_{eμ} D_μ; the manuscript's intermediate claims C(u) = √(h/12) I and C(r_μ) = (√ϑ/2) D_μ ⊗ I are the lemmas pulledCoefficient_uniformEnv / pulledCoefficient_contrastEnv; the 'determines the represented system algebra and commutant' clause is encoded as ten_panel_determines_coefficients (equal ten pullbacks ⇒ equal coefficient families, hence equal algebra and commutant). Scoped hypotheses (disclosed): H_E and H_R are finite-dimensional matrix spaces; 0 < ϑ < 1; only the parts of eq:six-edge-normalization / eq:helmert-frame that the proof uses are assumed (Σ_e K_e = √2 I, Σ_a Q_a = I, Oᵀ𝟏 = 0, OOᵀ = I − 𝟏𝟏ᵀ/6), so the theorem is slightly more general than stated; the Helmert frame O is a parameter. |
| `prop:reconstruction-error` | proposition | Robust commutant stopping test | [`RenewalGeometry.TenPanel.reconstruction_error`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.starClosedStack_commutator_perturbation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.robust_commutant_stopping_test`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.robust_commutant_stopping_test_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.helmert_sum_norm_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.l2_opNorm_le_matrixL2_norm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/TenPanelReconstructionExact.lean) | TenPanel.reconstruction_error proves all three clauses in the ten-panel frame: with measured panels X̂_μ, Ŷ_μ of Hilbert–Schmidt errors ε_{X,μ}, ε_{Y,μ} and the linear reconstruction D̂_μ = (2√3/√(ϑh))(X̂_μ + iŶ_μ), K̂_e = I/√18 + Σ_μ O_{eμ} D̂_μ (reconstructedCoefficient), (i) (Σ_e ‖K̂_e − K_e ⊗ I_R‖²_HS)^{1/2} ≤ e_K := (2√3/√(ϑh))(Σ_μ (ε_{X,μ}+ε_{Y,μ})²)^{1/2} (eq:appendix-reconstruction-error, via helmert_sum_norm_sq: OᵀO = I preserves the sum of squared errors); (ii) ‖(𝒟̂ − 𝒟)x‖ ≤ 2√2 e_K ‖x‖ for the star-closed stacked commutator maps jointCommutatorL2 (starClosedStack ·) (eq:appendix-commutator-error, via ‖A‖_op ≤ ‖A‖_HS and the existing jointCommutatorL2_sub_norm_le); (iii) if 𝒟̂ has least singular value ≥ ŝ on a protected complement W (ŝ‖x‖ ≤ ‖𝒟̂x‖ for x ∈ W) and ŝ > 2√2 e_K, then no nonzero x ∈ W lies in ker 𝒟 (generic versions: robust_commutant_stopping_test also gives the lower bound (ŝ − 2√2 e_K)‖x‖ ≤ ‖𝒟x‖ and the matrix form; robust_commutant_stopping_test_ker gives ker 𝒟 = M when W = Mᗮ for a verified protected M ⊆ ker 𝒟). Disclosed conventions: the reconstruction lives on the common carrier E × R, so the true coefficient compared against K̂_e is K_e ⊗ I_R (the paper writes K_e); the 'measured least singular value ŝ on the protected complement' is formalised as the bound ŝ‖x‖ ≤ ‖𝒟̂x‖ on W (equivalently ŝ ≤ σ_min(𝒟̂\|_W)); hypotheses as in prop:ten-panel-reconstruction plus OᵀO = I. |
| `thm:word-module-recognition` | proposition | Word-module recognition of historical internal actions | [`RenewalGeometry.word_module_recognition`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.word_module_recognition_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.word_module_recognition_coefficient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rightMultiplications`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.symmetryOperators`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.invariantElements`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.symmetry_mul_mulLeft`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.eq_mulLeft_of_mem_centralizer_rightMultiplications`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.mulLeft_mem_centralizer_symmetry_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleRecognition.lean) | Proved, and more generally than stated: for any unital C-algebra A (no finite-dimensionality, C*-structure or Wedderburn form needed; the statement is purely algebraic) and any family of algebra automorphisms alpha : G → (A ≃ₐ[C] A) (no group structure or finiteness of G needed), on the regular word carrier W = A with L_a = LinearMap.mulLeft, R_b = LinearMap.mulRight, U_g = (alpha g).toLinearMap: word_module_recognition states R(A)' ∩ U(G)' = L(A^G) as an equality of subsets of Module.End C A (commutants are Set.centralizer, A^G = invariantElements alpha = {a \| ∀ g, alpha g a = a}, which is the fixed-point form of A ∩ {u_g}' for alpha_g = Ad(u_g)); word_module_recognition_iff is the 'equivalently' clause (T commutes with every R_b and every U_g iff T is left multiplication by a unique invariant element) and word_module_recognition_coefficient gives the coefficient T 1. The Hilbert-space structure of L^2(A, tau_reg) plays no role in the theorem and is not encoded (the carrier is the underlying vector space). |
| `prop:word-module-defect` | proposition | Exact module and symmetry defects | [`RenewalGeometry.word_module_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.word_module_defect_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.word_module_invariant_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.defects_vanish_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.groupAverage_identity`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hsNorm_mulLeft_sq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hsNorm_sq_eq_sum`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.moduleDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.symmetryDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.groupAverage`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.hsNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.IsRegularTraceInner`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/WordModuleDefect.lean) | Proved. Setting: A a nontrivial finite-dimensional normed star ring over C with an inner product satisfying IsRegularTraceInner (<x,y> = tau_reg(x^* y) with tau_reg(z) = D^{-1} Tr_A(L_z), the intrinsic normalized regular trace formula of normalizedRegularTrace, equal to D^{-1} sum n_beta Tr(z_beta) in Wedderburn form); symmetries U_g = alpha_g given by a group hom alpha : G ->* (A ≃ₗᵢ[C] A) with alpha_g(1) = 1 (the paper's Ad(u_g) by unitaries is a special case, since tau_reg is tracial: regularTraceForm_mul_comm). hsNorm is the Hilbert-Schmidt norm (l^2 norm of (S e_nu) over the standard orthonormal basis), proved basis-independent via LinearMap.trace_eq_sum_inner (hsNorm_sq_eq_sum). word_module_defect: \|\|T - L_{T1}\|\|_HS = delta_R(T) (eq:word-module-defect, also squared form); groupAverage_identity: \|G\|^{-1} sum_g \|\|alpha_g a - a\|\|^2 = 2\|\|a - E_G a\|\|^2; hsNorm_mulLeft_sq: \|\|L_b\|\|_HS^2 = D\|\|b\|\|^2; word_module_invariant_defect: \|\|T - L_{E_G(T1)}\|\|_HS <= delta_R(T) + sqrt(D/2) delta_G(T) (eq:word-module-invariant-defect); defects_vanish_iff: both defects vanish iff T = L_a with a G-invariant (the 'Thus' clause). Scoped hypotheses disclosed: Nontrivial A (D != 0), Nonempty G in the vanishing clause, alpha_g isometric and unital rather than inner. |

| Proved in the scoped form recommended by the assessor: the corollary's mathematical content is witnessed on the paper's own twelve-root benchmark (Appendix prop:word-space-inflation: E = directed edges of K_4, S_4 relabeling matrices Pm, A = M_12). word_space_multiplicity_firewall: (i) the fixed points of the conjugation representation conjRep sigma X = P X P^{-1} on the word space L^2(M_12) are exactly the invariant algebra A^{S_4} = commutantSubmodule, so the trivial-representation multiplicity on the word space is finrank = 7 (commutant_finrank_eq_seven); (ii) the trivial multiplicity on the exposed physical carrier C^E is 1 (fixed vectors = constants, via S_4-transitivity on directed edges proved by decide); hence 'multiplicity on L^2(A) need not equal multiplicity on the physical carrier'; (iii) the word-space commutant contains an M_7(C) block: m7Block is an injective multiplicative linear embedding of Matrix (Fin 7) (Fin 7) C into End(L^2(M_12)) commuting with every conjRep sigma (built from the group average E_G and End(A^{S_4}) ≅ M_7); (iv) the physical invariant algebra A^{S_4} admits no injective linear image of M_3(C) (no_M3_embedding). Not formalised: the full multiplicity vectors (1,0,2,1,1) vs (7,5,19,17,12) for the non-trivial irreducibles (that is prop:word-space-inflation, a separate record) and the corollary's normative clauses ('must also pass thm:word-module-recognition', 'multiplicity-birth analysis refers to the physical carrier'), which are interpretive. |

Scope: the grading-changing variant of the cross block is the instance exhibited (the theorem is existential, so one instance suffices); the typed-incidence variant is not separately modelled. |

| `cor:historical-typed-compilation` | corollary | Historical typed-compilation criterion | [`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.historical_typed_compilation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.historical_typed_compilation_determined`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.external_shadow_not_compilable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.FlatWordTable`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.TableNativeShadow`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.TableNativeShadow.eval_compile`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.TableNativeShadow.comp_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.TableNativeShadow.groupAverage_apply`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.HistoricalTypedCompilation.ancestryShort`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/HistoricalTypedCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.external_shadow_no_go`](../../RenewalGeometry/Predictive/ExternalShadowNoGo.lean) | Proved as the criterion the paper's proof establishes. A flat historical word hierarchy is a FlatWordTable: a finite word bank of the historical *-algebra A with its multiplication table w_i w_j = sum_k t_ijk w_k and flatness (the bank spans A, so represented elements and finite functional-calculus elements p(a) are bank combinations); the word Gram is G_ij = tau(w_i^* w_j) for the inner product <y,x> = tau(y^* x) (tau = tau_reg gives the Grand-Tensor Gram). Typed shadows are TableNativeShadow: word-native maps X -> sum c_a A_a X B_a (def:word-native-shadow) whose insertions are declared in the bank; compressions by represented typing projectors, finite symmetry/router averages and compositions are table native (compression, groupAverage, comp). The compiler `compile` reduces every inserted product in the table (eval_compile). historical_typed_compilation: (1) the ordinary, ordinary-shadow and shadow-shadow blocks of the complete mixed ancestry Gram equal explicit functions gramForm of the word Gram, the table and the declared data; (2) the Schur ancestry residual H - C^* G^dagger C (ancestryShort, spectral Moore-Penrose inverse, the display eq:table-native-short) equals its compiled form; (3) the alternating determinant shadow DetIncidence.alt of an incidence tensor compiled entrywise from typed Gram blocks equals its value on compiled entries. historical_typed_compilation_determined: two hierarchies on different algebras with the same table, Gram and declared data give the same Gram, residual and alternating shadow (no independent cross-Hankel row). Converse: external_shadow_not_compilable restates thm:external-shadow-no-go (no function of the word-Gram hierarchy returns the external cross block). Disclosed renderings: 'represented or functional-calculus elements' = bank combinations; the Store-Clifford and matter-incidence shadows = arbitrary table-native shadows; the incidence tensor entering the alternating shadow is taken entrywise from typed Gram blocks. Re-verified 30 Sep 2026: the new version adds that the same table computes the six naturality defects of thm:six-naturality when their amplitudes and tensor-leg actions are historical (and defers mixed branches to prop:naturality-compilation). Disclosed: this clause is an instance of the shadow-shadow block clause of historical_typed_compilation with the defect maps R_X, S_Y as TableNativeShadow (they are word-native maps X -> a X - X b when the tensor-leg actions are bank elements) and the amplitudes as bank words, so d_nat = sum of shadow-shadow Gram diagonal entries is table-compiled; no separate Lean statement names the naturality defects, and the mixed-branch pointer is not a claim of this corollary. |
| `thm:connected-router` | proposition | Connected-router full-matrix theorem | [`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.adjoin_eq_top_iff_connected`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.adjoin_eq_top_of_connected`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.connected_of_adjoin_eq_top`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.single_mem_of_reachable`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.disconnected_block_reduction`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.blockProj_commute_of_mem_adjoin`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ConnectedRouter.diagProj_mul_mul_diagProj_ne_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ConnectedRouterExact.lean) | Proved on Matrix n n C for any finite index type n with decidable equality (nonempty for the direction full-algebra => connected, since Mathlib's SimpleGraph.Connected includes nonemptiness). C*(D_n,u) is encoded as the unital subalgebra Algebra.adjoin C (D_n ∪ {u, uᴴ}); the support graph is SimpleGraph.fromRel (u i j ≠ 0), and diagProj_mul_mul_diagProj_ne_zero_iff identifies the paper's edge condition p_i u p_j ≠ 0 with u i j ≠ 0. adjoin_eq_top_iff_connected is the boxed equivalence eq:router-full-matrix (walk induction single_mem_of_reachable + top_of_units); disconnected_block_reduction gives, for a disconnected support graph, a nonempty proper adjacency-closed component whose block projection commutes with the generated algebra (reducing subspace) and a proper block-diagonal subalgebra containing the generated algebra. |
| `cor:one-router-minimality` | corollary | One-router source minimality | [`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.one_router_source_minimality`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.cyclicRouter_generates`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.cyclicRouter_not_normal`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.diagonal_generated_commutative`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.cyclicRouter`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.diagProj`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneRouter.routerAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/OneRouterMinimality.lean) | Proved with an explicit router on an n-dimensional carrier Fin n with diagonal minimal projections p_i = E_ii: the nilpotent cyclic shift N (N_{i,i+1} = 1, support graph the path 1-2-...-n) satisfies (i) StarAlgebra.adjoin C {p_i, N} = top = M_n(C) for every n (cyclicRouter_generates; matrix units built from p_i N p_{i+1} along the path, adjoints for the reverse direction, then WeakReset.top_of_units); (ii) N is nonnormal for n >= 2 (cyclicRouter_not_normal, N N^H != N^H N); (iii) the star algebra generated by any set of diagonal matrices, in particular by the p_i alone (no off-diagonal word), is commutative (diagonal_generated_commutative via Algebra.commute_of_mem_adjoin_of_forall_mem_commute). one_router_source_minimality packages (i)-(iii) for n = m + 2. Scope: the manuscript's 'cyclic nonnormal router' is instantiated by this nilpotent shift (sufficiency is an existential claim, so one router instance suffices); the general connected-router criterion is thm:connected-router, not needed here. |

Colour: delta_col(u) = \|\|p_H u p_T\|\|_HS^2 + \|\|p_T u p_H\|\|_HS^2 on C^3 = T + H_priv is identified with omegaCol of InternalCommutantSeedExact; with the thm:colour-bridge generators (p_T, the four private matrix units = the hypothesis that private words generate M_2(H_priv), u, u*), the generated algebra equals the reducing block algebra M_2 + C (InternalSeed.blockDiag) iff delta_col = 0, and equals M_3 iff delta_col > 0. Weak: for p = \|t><t\|, q = \|h><h\| with unit t, h in C^2, delta_wk = \|\|[p,q]\|\|_HS^2 = 2c^2(1-c^2), c = \|<t,h>\| (from the all-vector polynomial identity \|\|[p,q]\|\|^2 = 2\|<t,h>\|^2 \|\|t\|\|^2\|\|h\|\|^2 - 2\|<t,h>\|^4); delta_wk > 0 iff p != q and <t,h> != 0 (p = q iff c^2 = 1, via the 2D Lagrange identity \|t0 h1 - t1 h0\|^2 + \|<t,h>\|^2 = \|\|t\|\|^2\|\|h\|\|^2), and then p, q generate M_2(C) (WeakReset.two_lines_generate). Norms are encoded entrywise (sum of \|entries\|^2); unit vectors as sum of normSq = 1. |
| `thm:subdirect-classification` | proposition | Finite subdirect matrix-algebra classification | [`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.finite_subdirect_classification`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.exists_wedderburn_pi_matrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.exists_factor_algEquiv_of_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.algEquiv_matrix_size_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.piMatrixBlockDiagonalAlgHom`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.SubdirectClassification.imageStarSubalgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/Commutant/SubdirectMatrixClassification.lean) | Proved (finite_subdirect_classification). Setting: B a unital star-closed subalgebra (Subalgebra + star_mem hypothesis) of the product ring Pi_{i<r} M_{n_i}(C), n_i >= 1, all coordinate projections surjective. Conclusion: there are k classes with sizes d_beta >= 1, a surjective class function cls : Fin r -> Fin k with n_i = d_{cls i} (coordinates in one class have equal matrix size; distinct sizes are never identified), and invertible V_i in M_{n_i}(C) such that x in B iff exists Y in Pi_beta M_{d_beta}(C) with x_i = V_i Y_{cls i} V_i^{-1} for all i (Y_{cls i} read in M_{n_i} through n_i = d_{cls i} via Matrix.reindex (finCongr _)); i.e. B is the diagonal embedding of one independent full matrix algebra per class, up to the inner automorphisms Ad(V_i). Infrastructure built: exists_wedderburn_pi_matrix (intrinsic Wedderburn decomposition B = Pi_beta M_{d_beta}(C) for any star-closed subalgebra of Pi_i M_{n_i}(C): transport along the block-diagonal embedding to FiniteComplexStarSubalgebraSemisimplicity.starSubalgebra_isSemisimpleRing, then Mathlib's IsSemisimpleRing.exists_algEquiv_pi_matrix_of_isAlgClosed), exists_factor_algEquiv_of_surjective (a surjective unital hom Pi_beta M_{d_beta} -> M_n kills all summands but one and is an isomorphism on it: images of the central units are scalar idempotents summing to 1; RingHom.injective out of the simple ring M_d), algEquiv_matrix_size_eq (isomorphic full matrix algebras have equal size), and NCG.matrix_algEquiv_inner (Skolem-Noether) for the inner form. Scoping: n_i >= 1 (paper's implicit nondegeneracy of the displayed factors); C*(B) is rendered as the unital *-closed subalgebra; 'up to inner automorphisms' is rendered with invertible (not necessarily unitary) conjugators V_i, which is what the paper's proof (Skolem-Noether) supplies. |
| `thm:reciprocal-return` | proposition | Finite reciprocal-return criterion | [`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.reciprocal_return_criterion`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.Letter`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.letterAct`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.wordAct`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.HasBridge`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.word_hasBridge`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.partialGram_pow_mul_eq_zero_all`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.word_eq_zero_all`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.reducingSubspace`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.reducingSubspace_invariant`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnBank.no_bridge_of_mass_eq_zero`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnBankExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_pos_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.word`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean) | Proved (reciprocal_return_criterion) at the paper's horizon n = dim N, for a finite group G with a unitary representation v (v(gg') = v g v g', v 1 = 1, v_g^* v_g = 1) whose commutant is scalar (Schur form of irreducibility), h self-adjoint, all index types finite, V nonempty. The declared route bank is encoded as words (lists) in the letters A_g = (v_g (x) I)A, A_g^*, B_g^* = B^*(v_g^* (x) I), B_g, D = I (x) h, and arbitrary operations inside T and inside H_priv (the represented private support words), acting on T + (V (x) N) + H_priv; a word 'has a colour bridge' when its H_priv <- T corner is nonzero (HasBridge). Established: the boxed identity m_ret = d^-1 Tr(rho_B W_A) and m_ret >= 0; (i) <=> (ii) <=> (iii) <=> (iv), where (iv) is 'rho_B h^k rho_A != 0 for some k' (all k in N, equivalently k < n by Cayley-Hamilton, partialGram_pow_mul_eq_zero_all), i.e. Ran rho_B not orthogonal to the h-cyclic space of Ran rho_A; when the conditions fail, the explicit subspace T + K + 0 with K = cap_{g,k} Ker B^*(v_g^* (x) h^k) (it contains V (x) Ran W_A) is invariant under every letter of the bank -- the letter set is closed under adjoints, so this is a reducing subspace -- contains T and is annihilated by the H_priv coordinate, so no word of the bank has a bridge; when they hold, the witness B_1^* D^k A_g has k + 2 <= n + 1 letters. Disclosed gap: the paper's reducing projection is onto T + (V (x) Ran W_A); the Lean subspace is the (possibly larger) invariant subspace of mediator vectors from which no return word reaches H_priv, which serves the same purpose. |
| `cor:reciprocal-return-force` | corollary | Port-relative reachability forces colour | [`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.reciprocalReturn_force`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_eq`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturn.trace_partialGram_mul_ge`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMargin.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ReciprocalReturn.trace_partialGram`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMargin.lean) | Proved unconditionally: reciprocalReturn_force derives both boxed bounds (m_ret >= (kappa/d)\|B\|^2_HS and max_{g,k<n} \|r_{g,k}\|^2_HS >= kappa/(d n) \|B\|^2_HS) and B != 0 => m_ret > 0 from W_A >= kappa I, using the mass identity mass_eq of thm:reciprocal-return (now proved) instead of taking it as a hypothesis; the trace-monotonicity and partial-Gram lemmas of ReciprocalReturnMargin.lean are reused. Scoped: finite index types, V nonempty, n > 0, v a unitary representation of the finite group G with scalar commutant, h self-adjoint. Caveat for the ledger: ReciprocalReturnMargin.reciprocalReturn_margin is stated for ReciprocalReturn.returnWord = B^* (v g ⊗ₖ h ^ k) A, which by Mathlib precedence is B^*(v_g^k ⊗ h^k)A; the new file uses the paper's word B^*(v_g ⊗ h^k)A (ReciprocalReturnMass.word) and re-derives the margin argument for it. The 'two-leg colour return for a multiplicity-one mediator' remark and the link to thm:colour-bridge are not separately formalised. |

walGeometry.ReciprocalReturnMass.mass_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/ReciprocalReturnMassExact.lean) | Proved. all_excursion_return_of_mass_eq_zero: in the setting of thm:reciprocal-return (carrier T + H_priv + (V x N), covariant entries (v_g x I)A, reverse exits B*(v_g* x I), mediator I x h with h Hermitian, internal operations on T and H_priv, plus adjoints; words = generated multiplicative monoid), if m_ret = 0 at the horizon n = dim N then (1) N_A = Span{h^k Ran rho_A : k < n} and N_B = Span{h^k Ran rho_B : k < n} (cyclicSpace, subspaces of EuclideanSpace C N) are orthogonal (Submodule.IsOrtho), (2) the stopping projection Q = I_T + 0 + (I_V x P_{N_A}) with P_{N_A} the orthogonal projection onto N_A as a matrix (cyclicProj := toEuclideanLin.symm of starProjection) commutes with every generator of the bank and its adjoint, and (3) every word w has p_H w p_T = 0. Ingredients: Cayley-Hamilton gives h^k Ran rho in N_rho for every k (pow_mul_apply_mem), hence h-invariance; h self-adjoint makes the projection commute with h (starProjection_comm_of_invariant); P rho_A = rho_A; m_ret = 0 <-> rho_B h^k rho_A = 0 (mass_eq_zero_iff) gives P rho_B = 0 and N_A perp N_B. Scoped hypotheses as in ReciprocalReturnMassExact: finite index types, V nonempty, v a unitary representation of the finite group G irreducible in the Schur (scalar-commutant) form, h Hermitian. |

Scoped hypothesis: [Nonempty n] (as in thm:connected-router, since SimpleGraph.Connected requires it). |
| `thm:finite-internal-landing-alternative` | proposition | Finite internal landing alternative | [`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.finite_internal_landing_alternative`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.landing_flat_obstruction`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.landing_reducible_colour`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.landing_commutative_weak`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.landing_saturation`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.router_iff_weakResidual_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.FiniteLanding.bridgeFamilyGens`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/FiniteInternalLandingExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.central_separation_dichotomy`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.lockedAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.finrank_lockedAlgebra`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CentralSeparation.etaCen_pos_of_central_projector`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/CentralSeparationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.InternalDeficit.internal_deficit_alternatives`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean) | Re-verified 30 Sep 2026. The new version no longer asserts mutual exclusivity ('the following finite implications apply'), which is exactly the Lean rendering (finite_internal_landing_alternative = conjunction of the four branch implications with their finite witnesses plus the dimension table). (A1) = cor:flat-multiplicity-obstruction with n = 3 (FlatMultiplicityObstruction.lean; general external type lambda, external-trivial type the special case): at a flat depth with m_{lambda,r} < 3 no injective linear map / unital hom M_3(C) -> End(M_lambda(V_s)) exists at any later depth. (A2) on T + H_priv: if every represented bridge u in a family has delta_col(u) = 0, Algebra.adjoin of p_T, the private units and all u, u^* is the reducible block algebra M_2 + C (InternalSeed.blockDiag). (A3) if all pairs of represented rank-one carriers on the weak plane have delta_wk = 0 the generated algebra is commutative. (A4) on the complete census m_r = (3,0,2,1,1) carrier C^3 + C^2 + C + C of InternalDeficitAlternativesExact.lean: a colour-supported bridge with omega7 > 0 and a unit weak router h with delta_wk(e_3,h) > 0 (router_iff_weakResidual_pos = Router h) give Algebra.adjoin(baseGens, u, u^*, \|h><h\|) = the full block algebra M_3 + M_2 + C + C of dimension 15 (landing_saturation); the new hypothesis 'the two scalar characters are centrally separated' is realized by the independent scalar central supports E_55, E_66 in baseGens (CentralSeparation.etaCen_pos_of_central_projector); the identification of this block algebra with A'_ext at the census (thm:active-isotypic) is not part of the statement. Dimension table 9/11/13/15 = InternalDeficit.internal_deficit_alternatives; the new remark that without scalar central separation the blockwise-surjective terminal branch may have dimension fourteen is CentralSeparation.central_separation_dichotomy / lockedAlgebra / finrank_lockedAlgebra. |
| `thm:rectangular-normalization-kernel` | proposition | Exact rectangular normalization kernel | [`RenewalGeometry.rectangularNormalization`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rectangularNormalization_surjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rectangularNormalization_finrank_ker`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rectangularNormalization_finrank_ker_add`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.rectangularNormalization_injective_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.partialTraceRight_kronecker_one`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/RectangularNormalizationKernelExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.partialTraceRight`](../../RenewalGeometry/Operational/SpectatorProduct.lean) | Proved. Encoding: recorded branches are grouped by source sector (Br sigma = branches with source A_sigma, i.e. the fibres of s : A -> S; this is the Sigma-type form of the source map and avoids dependent casts), p_sigma = card (Src sigma), q_b = card (T sigma b); the amplitude coefficient matrices X_b live on Src sigma x T sigma b (source (x) target order) and Tr_{T_b} is partialTraceRight. rectangularNormalization is the linear map Gamma_rect((X_b)) = (+)_sigma sum_{b : Br sigma} Tr_{T_b} X_b (eq:rectangular-normalization-map; the map is taken as the definition, as in the paper's first sentence of proof, since the matrix-unit product identity B^*_{a,i alpha} B_{a,j beta} = delta_{ij} \|e_alpha><e_beta\| is the coefficient convention fixed in the preceding text). rectangularNormalization_surjective: surjective onto (+)_sigma M_{p_sigma}(C) when every source sector occurs in a branch with nonzero-dimensional target (lift q^{-1} Y (x) 1_T on one branch, partialTraceRight_kronecker_one). rectangularNormalization_finrank_ker: dim_C Ker = sum_sigma p_sigma^2 (sum_{b} q_b^2 - 1) (eq:rectangular-normalization-nullity, natural-number subtraction, well defined since sum_b q_b^2 >= 1; the subtraction-free rank-nullity form is rectangularNormalization_finrank_ker_add), via LinearMap.finrank_range_add_finrank_ker and Module.finrank_pi_fintype/finrank_matrix. rectangularNormalization_injective_iff: with positive sector and target dimensions and every sector occurring (the paper's implicit 'all q_a are positive integers'), injective <-> every sector has exactly one branch (card (Br sigma) = 1) and that branch has one-dimensional target. |
| `lem:weak-naturality-spectrum` | lemma | Weak defect spectrum | [`RenewalGeometry.weakDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakLPlus`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakLMinus`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakDefect_charpoly`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakDefect_mulVec_eq_zero_iff`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakDefect_transpose_charpoly`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakDefect_isHermitian`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakAlternating`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakLPlus_mulVec_alternating`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.weakLMinus_mulVec_alternating`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/WeakNaturalitySpectrumExact.lean) | Proved. F_12 = \|w_1><w_2\|, F_21 = \|w_2><w_1\| are the 2x2 matrix units in the orthonormal basis (w_1, w_2) (eq:six-naturality-generators), L_+/- = F_{12/21} (x) I + I (x) F_{12/21} on C^2 (x) C^2 (Kronecker on Fin 2 x Fin 2), H_W = L_+ L_- + L_- L_+ (weakDefect). weakDefect_charpoly: the characteristic polynomial is X (X - 2)^2 (X - 4), i.e. spec H_W = {0^(1), 2^(2), 4^(1)} with multiplicities (eq:weak-naturality-spectrum); weakDefect_isHermitian (so the algebraic multiplicities are the spectral ones). weakDefect_mulVec_eq_zero_iff: the zero eigenspace is exactly the alternating weak covector line C (w_1 (x) w_2 - w_2 (x) w_1) (weakAlternating; both ladders annihilate it, weakLPlus/LMinus_mulVec_alternating). The 'dual-space version' is the transpose: weakDefect_transpose shows H_W^T = H_W and weakDefect_transpose_charpoly gives the same spectrum. The computation goes through the explicit product-basis matrix diag(2, [[2,2],[2,2]], 2) (weakDefect_apply, charpoly_reindex). |

A normalized instrument is a finite Kraus family with sum_b K_b^H K_b = 1 and P_alt is any nonzero idempotent matrix (the paper's I_C (x) \|a><a\| = T_0^H T_0 is one). branch_weight_eq_one: if the distinguished branch has effect x . P_alt (eq:naturality-ray with the partial-isometry normalization) and every other amplitude satisfies K P_alt = 0, then x = 1 (right-compression of the trace-preservation identity by P_alt); branch_weight_eq_one_of_amplitude gives the amplitude form K_0 = c T_0, \|c\|^2 = 1. competing_branch_weights_sum_eq_one: for a set S of branches with effects x_a . P_alt and all others annihilating P_alt, sum_{a in S} x_a = 1. finrank_scalarNormalizationKernel: the linear scalar normalization kernel {x : sum x_a = 0} of k >= 1 branches has dimension k - 1, and sub_mem_scalarNormalizationKernel shows the solution set of sum x_a = 1 is a coset of it. Scoped: x taken in C (the paper's x >= 0 is a special case); positivity x_a >= 0 is a remark in the proof, not a claim. |

End), tensorWeight = slot sums on the diagonal k-fold carrier. determinant_arity_floor: support ⊆ {(0,0),±(1,0),±(0,1),±(1,−1)} (equality when N nonempty), (−3,−2) absent from the single-system support, absent on k-fold carriers for k<5 (total charge bound a,b≥0, a+b≤k), present at k=5 (line C⊗C⊗C⊗W₂⊗W₂ of weight (3,2) → neutral line). torusAction_conj_single proves that conjugating the matrix unit E_ij by the diagonal torus action diag(e^{i(aα+bβ)}) multiplies it by the character of w i − w j, so endWeightSupport is the actual central character support of the matrix units; det_weight_matrix_unit exhibits the k=5 rank-one map with character (−3,−2). No downgrade. |
| `prop:naturality-compilation` | proposition | Finite functional and word-moment realization | [`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.trace_determined_of_mem_span`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.naturality_defect_and_trace_determined`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.trace_determined_modulo_constants`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.naturalityOperator`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.amplitudeChoi`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.naturalityDefect`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.trace_gram_mul_vecMulVec`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.NaturalityCompilation.word_naturality_defect`](../../RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/NaturalityCompilationExact.lean) | Proved. Contextual route: trace_determined_of_mem_span shows that for H in the real span of the tested functionals F_l, Tr(F_l J) = Tr(F_l J') for all l implies Tr(H J) = Tr(H J') (span induction; J_D itself is never used, so the bank determines the functional without determining the Choi operator); naturality_defect_and_trace_determined applies it to H_nat and I giving d_nat and Tr J_D; trace_determined_modulo_constants is the refinement with H = H_0 + c, H_0 in the span and c constant on the declared feasible class. Word-moment route: with H_nat = sum_X R_X^H R_X + sum_Y S_Y^H S_Y (eq:naturality-operator) and J_D = sum_a \|T_a>><<T_a\| (amplitudeChoi), word_naturality_defect proves d_nat = Tr(H_nat J_D) = sum_a (sum_X \|\|R_X T_a\|\|^2_HS + sum_Y \|\|S_Y T_a\|\|^2_HS) (eq:word-naturality-defect). Scoped rendering: the port is a finite coordinate space p -> C (the amplitude space in Hilbert-Schmidt coordinates), the inserted actions R_X, S_Y are matrices on it, and \|\|.\|\|^2_HS is the coordinate sum of normSq. The sentences 'at flatness their multiplication table computes it exactly' (thm:inserted-word-moments, already proved) and the trace-convention remarks are interpretive and not restated. |

With P_K a Hermitian idempotent (P^2 = P, P^H = P) and K = Ran P_K (carrier), C_{F\|K} := U^H (1 - P_K) U (completenessResidual): fermion_source_completeness bundles C = ((1-P)U)^H ((1-P)U) >= 0 (PSD); C = 0 <=> Ran U <= K (eq:fermion-source-completeness-zero, first equivalence); Ran U <= K <=> for all A in A_F, A(Ran U) <= K for any set A_F containing I and leaving K invariant (second equivalence, sourceRange_le_carrier_iff_invariant); Tr C = \|\|(1-P_K)U\|\|^2_HS (completenessResidual_trace, HS norm as sum of normSq); and rank C + dim(Ran U ∩ K) = dim Ran U (completenessResidual_rank_add, by rank-nullity for (1-P)\|_{Ran U} and rank(V^H V) = rank V), which is eq:fermion-source-completeness-rank.
