| Proved on the explicit carrier Fin 15 = Q(6)+u(3)+d(3)+L(2)+e(1) (sector15): with the gauge algebra encoded by all matrix units inside each sector (the paper's A_gauge = M6+M3+M3+M2+C from irreducible pairwise inequivalent modules) and three incidence matrices Y_e supported on the sector blocks Q x u, Q x d, L x e and nonzero, Algebra.adjoin C (gauge units, Y_e, Y_e*) equals the block algebra of the quark/lepton labelling (fibres of 12 and 3 vertices, i.e. M_12 + M_3), every element of its centralizer is diag(a.1_12, b.1_3) and the centralizer has finrank 2 (= C^2). The neutral-singlet variant on Fin 16 with a fourth nonzero L x nu incidence gives M_12 + M_4 with the same C^2 commutant. The engine is general: for any sectors beta : n -> B, incidence family Y and labelling kappa constant on sectors and incidence supports, adjoin = block algebra of kappa iff-style (adjoin_eq_labelAlgebra, via matrix units along walks of the vertex support graph), the commutant of a block algebra is the block-scalar diagonals with dimension = number of blocks, and reachability is transferred from the finite sector pattern graph (decided by `decide`). |
| `cor:incidence-one-sided-rank` | corollary | One-sided operator--Schmidt rank certificate | [`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.hsNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.sliceMatrix`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.sliceOperator`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.schmidtCoefficient`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.schmidtRank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.opNorm_euclideanOperatorOfMatrix_le_hsNorm`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.norm_sliceOperator_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.schmidtCoefficient_lower`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.schmidtRank_lower`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.OneSidedRank.incidence_one_sided_rank`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/OneSidedRankCertificateExact.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.approximationSingularValue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ApproximationSingularValues.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.approximationSingularValue_lower_perturbation`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ApproximationSingularValues.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.approximationSingularValue_eq_zero_of_rank_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/ApproximationSingularValues.lean) | Proved, in the approximation-number rendering of singular values. An incidence Y in B(K_s,K_t) (x) B(M_s,M_t) is a matrix Y : Matrix (Kt x Mt) (Ks x Ms) C; its slice map S_Y (prop:incidence-slice-Gram) is the realigned matrix sliceMatrix Y as a Euclidean operator (sliceOperator), sigma_{k+1}(Y) := approximationSingularValue (S_Y) k (Eckart-Young rendering: operator-norm distance to rank <= k) and rank Y := rank of S_Y (the operator-Schmidt rank). incidence_one_sided_rank: if hsNorm (Yhat - Y) <= eps (\|\|.\|\|_HS = sqrt of the sum of \|entries\|^2) then for all k, max(sigmahat_k - eps, 0) <= sigma_k(Y), and for every N, #{k < N : sigmahat_k > eps} <= rank Y (eq:incidence-one-sided-rank). Ingredients proved here: the operator norm of a matrix is at most its Hilbert-Schmidt norm (row-wise Cauchy-Schwarz, opNorm_euclideanOperatorOfMatrix_le_hsNorm), realignment preserves the HS norm, hence \|\|S_Yhat - S_Y\|\|_op <= \|\|Yhat - Y\|\|_HS (norm_sliceOperator_sub_le); the count uses antitonicity of the approximation numbers and 'rank <= k => sigma_{k+1} = 0'. The final caveat (a coefficient below the error floor cannot certify absence) is a remark, not a claim; the identification of the approximation numbers with the classical singular values of the SVD is the disclosed rendering (shared with thm:incidence-slice-cluster). |
| `prop:compact-mosco-resolvent` | proposition | Compact Mosco convergence implies norm-resolvent convergence | [`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.operatorGraphMosco_lowSpectrumConvergence_of_graphScreens`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/OperatorGraphMoscoLowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.orderedEigenvalues_spec_of_compact_isPositive`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.orderedEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.courantValue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.tendsto_courantValue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.finrank_eigenspace_eq_ncard`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.operatorGraphMosco_kernelSpectralConsequences_of_graphScreens`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/OperatorGraphMoscoKernelSpectralConsequencesFromGraphScreens.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.compressedOperator_tendsto_operatorNorm_of_graphScreens`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/GraphScreenCompressedResolventNormConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProjectionStability.eventually_finrank_range_eq_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/NearbyProjectionRankStability.lean) | Proved. Under cofinal Mosco convergence of the operator-graph energies with weak resolvent equations, asymptotic density and a compact graph-screen tail (the rendering of asymptotic form compactness), operatorGraphMosco_lowSpectrumConvergence_of_graphScreens gives: operator-norm convergence of the transported resolvents J_n R_n J_n^* -> R_b (eq:norm-resolvent), compactness and positivity of the limit resolvent, norm convergence of the kernel-cluster Riesz projections with eventually equal ranks (isolated-cluster clause; any isolated nonzero cluster via the existing one-shift spectral-gap files), and the ordered eigenvalue convergence mu_{k,X} -> mu_{k,infty} for every fixed k together with lambda_{k,X} = 1/mu_{k,X} - b -> lambda_{k,infty} whenever mu_{k,infty} > 0 (eq:eigenvalue-convergence). The ordered eigenvalues are the Courant-Fischer min-max values courantValue R k = sup_{dim V = k+1} inf_{x in V, \|x\|=1} re<Rx,x>; orderedEigenvalues_spec_of_compact_isPositive proves that for a compact positive operator these form a nonnegative antitone sequence, that for every lam > 0 the number of k with mu_k = lam equals the dimension of the lam-eigenspace, that every positive value is an eigenvalue and every positive eigenvalue occurs, i.e. (mu_k) is exactly the enumeration of the positive eigenvalues in decreasing order repeated with multiplicity (padded by 0 beyond the rank). Scope as in the earlier partial: the resolvent is taken at a positive shift b (R_b = (L + b)^{-1}, so lambda = 1/mu - b; the paper's b = 1), the stage resolvents are transported by the varying-Hilbert system J, and the Hilbert carrier is separable. |

| `lem:collective-compactness` | lemma | Asymptotic compactness gives collective compactness | [`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.exists_convergent_subseq_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.seqCollectivelyCompact_resolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.isCompactOperator_limitResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.IsTransportedResolvent`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.IsTransportedResolvent.energy_bound`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.SeqCollectivelyCompact`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.System.CollectivelyCompact.seqCollectivelyCompact`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.collectivelyCompact_of_seq_of_stage_compact`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.transportedObjective`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.VaryingHilbert.isCompactOperator_of_subseq`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/AsymptoticCollectiveCompactness.lean) | Proved in the form the paper's proof establishes, on the varying-Hilbert System carrier with def:asymptotic-compactness = System.AsymptoticallyCompact / LimitFormCompact (TransportedMoscoAsymptoticCompactness.lean). Resolvent vectors R_n f = (L_n+I)^{-1} J_n^* f are encoded as minimizers of the paper's objective F_{n,f}(u) = q_n(u) + \|\|u\|\|^2 - 2 Re<J_n u, f> (only the comparison with u = 0 is used, plus q_n(0) = 0 and R_n f in the form domain); this yields eq:resolvent-energy-bound as \|\|R_n f\|\|^2 + q_n(R_n f) <= 8\|\|f\|\|^2. Then (i) for bounded sources f_n the outputs J_n R_n f_n have a strongly convergent subsequence; (ii) the transported resolvents are sequentially collectively compact (one output per stage: SeqCollectivelyCompact, which is what the paper's proofs of this lemma and of lem:collective-norm use, and which is implied by the compact-container CollectivelyCompact of VaryingHilbertCollectiveCompactness.lean); (iii) the limiting resolvent J_inf R_inf is a compact operator (from LimitFormCompact). Disclosed gap: the compact-container form of collective compactness does not follow from the paper's one-member-per-stage definition alone (counterexample q_X = X\|\|.\|\|^2 on a fixed infinite-dimensional H: asymptotically compact, but no stage resolvent (X+1)^{-1} I is compact); collectivelyCompact_of_seq_of_stage_compact derives it under the extra hypotheses that the sequential property holds along every strictly monotone reindexing and every stage operator is compact. |

[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.lowSpectrumConvergence`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.tendsto_orderedEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.tendsto_shiftedInverse_orderedEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.abs_orderedEigenvalue_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.orderedEigenvalues_spec_of_compact_isPositive`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.LowSpectrum.orderedEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/LowSpectrumConvergence.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.courantValue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.abs_courantValue_sub_le`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.finrank_eigenspace_eq_ncard`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.hasEigenvalue_of_courantValue_pos`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.exists_courantValue_eq_of_hasEigenvalue`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.courantValue_antitone_of_isPositive`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.exists_orthonormal_eigenvectors`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.CourantFischer.exists_unit_eigenvector_of_sup_rayleigh`](../../RenewalGeometry/Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ResolventStability.circleRieszProjection_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/RieszProjectionStability.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ResolventStability.circleRieszProjection`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/RieszProjectionStability.lean)<br>[`RenewalGeometry.ProjectionStability.eventually_finrank_range_eq_of_tendsto`](../../RenewalGeometry/OperatorLimits/NearbyProjectionRankStability.lean) | Proved. New infrastructure (Analysis/CompactSelfAdjointMinMax.lean): the Courant-Fischer min-max values courantValue T k = sup_{dim V = k+1} inf_{x in V, \|x\|=1} re<Tx,x> of a bounded operator on an inner product space over R or C; the min-max principle gives \|mu_k(A) - mu_k(B)\| <= \|A - B\| (abs_courantValue_sub_le) and hence mu_k(R_X) -> mu_k(R_infty) under norm-resolvent convergence, exactly the paper's argument. Identification with the eigenvalues, proved without any spectral theorem: for a compact symmetric operator, a generalised Cauchy-Schwarz inequality for positive operators plus compactness produce a top eigenvector (exists_unit_eigenvector_of_sup_rayleigh), a greedy orthonormal eigenvector family e_i with T e_i = mu_i e_i and the quadratic form bounded by mu_n on the complement (exists_orthonormal_eigenvectors), and finally, for every lam > 0, #{k : mu_k = lam} = dim eigenspace(lam) (finrank_eigenspace_eq_ncard), every positive mu_k is an eigenvalue and every positive eigenvalue is some mu_k; for positive T the sequence is antitone and nonnegative. Thus mu_k is the k-th positive eigenvalue repeated with multiplicity (zero-indexed, padded by 0 beyond the rank of a finite-rank operator). lowSpectrumConvergence packages, for compact symmetric R_X -> R_infty in norm: mu_{k,X} -> mu_{k,infty} for every k; lambda_{k,X} = 1/mu_{k,X} - 1 -> lambda_{k,infty} whenever mu_{k,infty} > 0 (the limiting eigenvalue is finite); for every circle contour avoiding 0, in the resolvent set of R_infty and eventually of R_X, with uniform resolvent bounds on the contour, the Riesz projections Pi_X(Gamma) -> Pi_infty(Gamma) in operator norm and their ranks are eventually equal. Scope: the contour is a circle (the isolated finite cluster is the spectrum inside it), and the uniform resolvent bound on the contour is taken as a hypothesis as in the earlier partial. |

The prose alternatives 'supplied directly as an incidence or recovered relative to a marked neutral reference' are not part of the encoded object. | — |  |

On the carrier C^n with grading Gamma and typed resolution of the identity (P_t)_t, the represented relations are a declared set Rel of typed pairs (the fermionic Yukawa incidence relations determined by the matter packet, plus the declared Majorana pair when the neutral/charge-conjugate sectors are represented; Rel is packet data, required only to be symmetric). D_F := relationSpace Gamma P Rel is the Submodule of the Hilbert-Schmidt space EuclideanSpace C (n x n) (realized via matrixL2 / l2Matrix) of grading-odd operators Gamma X Gamma = -X whose typed blocks P_t X P_t' vanish for (t,t') not in Rel; it is star-closed for self-adjoint Gamma, P_t and symmetric Rel (relationSpace_star_mem) and finite-dimensional (instFiniteDimensional_relationSpace). Pi_F^D := relationProjection = Submodule.starProjection D_F (the HS orthogonal projection), D_F^can := canonicalFiniteDirac = l2Matrix (Pi (matrixL2 D~_F)) and Delta_F^rel := relationDefect = \|\|matrixL2 D~_F - Pi (matrixL2 D~_F)\|\|^2 (eq:finite-dirac-relation-defect), with D~_F = finiteDirac Gamma A of eq:finite-dirac. | — |  |

| `cor:source-native-endpoint-allocation` | corollary | Source-native endpoint allocation | medium | Depends on the encodings of thm:endpoint-ancestry-compiler (T_f = d_f^{-1} Tr_{V_f} Ξ̂_f) and of the endpoint allocation short a_{f\|G}; new file RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/SourceNativeEndpointAllocationExact.lean: on the zero-defect branch Ξ̂_f = 1 ⊗ₖ T_f with T_f : G_gen → N_f injective (T_f^*T_f ≻ 0), so Ran Ξ̂_f = V_f ⊗ Ran T_f and Ran Ξ_f = V_f ⊗ N_f ⟺ Ran T_f = N_f ⟺ dim N_f = dim G_gen = 3 (endpoint_generation_carrier gives dim G_gen = 3); a_{f\|G} = 0 ⟺ the Schur residual of the source against Ran Ξ_f vanishes (sourceSchurResidual_eq_zero_iff_rangeIncluded, ExactSourceSchurResidual.lean). Build on EquivariantMultiplicityFactorization.lean, EndpointGenerationCarrierExact.lean. |
| `cor:primitive-odd-alternative` | corollary | Primitive active no-matter or occurrence alternative | medium | First encode thm:odd-choi-occurrence (Choi matrix of a Kraus family, P_odd = (I − Θ_J)/2 on HS space, μ_J = Σ_α ‖(K_α)_odd‖²_HS with Kraus independence via the Choi matrix; TypedTransitionGeneratorAudit.oneKrausChoi and oneKrausChoi_mass_eq_hilbertSchmidtSq give the one-Kraus Choi/HS identity pattern). Then new file RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/PrimitiveOddAlternativeExact.lean: represent words as lists of letters acting by composition of CP maps; μ = 0 ⟺ every Kraus operator of every letter commutes with J (eq:odd-choi-zero, sum of squares); the composition of maps whose Kraus operators are all grading-even has grading-even Kraus operators (products commute with J), giving ⟸ by list induction; ⟹ is the one-letter case. The cross-sign support clause is the contrapositive of the sum-of-squares vanishing. |
| `cth:microscopic-odd-lift` | proposition | Microscopic odd mass is not fixed by visible dynamics | medium | New file RenewalGeometry/StandardModel/MicroscopicOddLiftExact.lean (after encoding μ_J as in cor:primitive-odd-alternative's plan): U_θ is unitary (cos² + sin² = 1, σ_x² = 1); the single Kraus operator of the lift is K = 1_d ⊗ₖ U_θ; its odd part w.r.t. Z̃ = Z_act ⊗ σ_z is 1_d ⊗ (i sin θ σ_x) because Z_act commutes with 1_d and σ_z anticommutes with σ_x (clockX_mul_Z-type lemmas of Lorentz/AnchorClifford.lean), so ‖K_odd‖²_HS = d · 2 sin²θ (trace of Kronecker product, ‖σ_x‖²_HS = 2); the visible marginal is the identity channel since Tr_S(Ad_{U_θ}(ρ ⊗ σ)) = ρ Tr σ, and multitime words factor the same way (partial trace of a Kronecker product). Use Matrix.trace_kronecker and explicit 2×2 computations. |
